Regarding contracted teachers: Contracted teachers from 2016 can become staffed teachers at the end of four years. This matter is guaranteed by the law. So we're not talking about how they're constantly in this position. The question was, "What is the number of teachers? " During the year 2018, the number of substitute teachers serving in the schools of our Ministry is ten. They serve in our provinces of Pain, Antalya, Bursa, Mersin, and Niğde. There was a question about the earthquake investigation of 21 schools in Sakarya. In 2015, according to our ministry, 4,500 schools across the country began their earthquake study. In this case, 125 schools in the province of Sakarya were subjected to earthquake inspections. The current situation regarding 21 schools that need to be strengthened is this: the decision to destroy nine buildings belonging to seven schools has been made. Our students in these schools have moved to other schools. Instead of these buildings destroyed, the construction of new educational buildings is part of the investment programme, and the philanthropists are doing it. Six buildings belonging to five schools have been reinforced. Pre-trial preparations for strengthening 10 buildings belonging to nine schools will be completed and will be conducted in 2019. There was a question about the need for a lecture to end bilateral education. The construction of 63,501 classrooms is still under way to end bilateral teaching throughout Turkey. The number of courses required to end bilateral education is 29,535. There have been 163,000 needs, and the real number is 29.535. The required classrooms are intended to be completed within two years and to transfer to single education. There was a question about the need for lectures in Gaziantep.
